=== ./src/crypto/mod.rs ===
// src/crypto/mod.rs
use chacha20poly1305::{
    aead::{Aead, KeyInit},
    XChaCha20Poly1305, XNonce,
};
use hkdf::Hkdf;
use rand::{rngs::OsRng, RngCore};
use sha2::Sha256;
use x25519_dalek::{PublicKey, StaticSecret};
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};

pub const NONCE_SIZE: usize = 24;
pub const TAG_SIZE: usize = 16;
pub const FRAME_HEADER_SIZE: usize = 14;
pub const MAGIC_BYTES_VALUE: u16 = 0x4E53;

pub const SIZE_TINY: usize = 256;
pub const SIZE_SMALL: usize = 1024;
pub const SIZE_MEDIUM: usize = 4096;
pub const SIZE_MAX: usize = 65536;

pub const ALLOWED_TOTAL_SIZES: [usize; 4] = [SIZE_TINY, SIZE_SMALL, SIZE_MEDIUM, SIZE_MAX];

// ECDH Key Exchange
pub fn generate_shared_key(my_private_key: &[u8], their_public_key: &[u8]) -> Result<[u8; 32]> {
    if my_private_key.len() != 32 {
        return Err(anyhow!("private key must be 32 bytes, got {}", my_private_key.len()));
    }
    if their_public_key.len() != 32 {
        return Err(anyhow!("public key must be 32 bytes, got {}", their_public_key.len()));
    }

    let mut private_bytes = [0u8; 32];
    private_bytes.copy_from_slice(my_private_key);
    let private_key = StaticSecret::from(private_bytes);

    let mut public_bytes = [0u8; 32];
    public_bytes.copy_from_slice(their_public_key);
    let public_key = PublicKey::from(public_bytes);

    let shared_secret = private_key.diffie_hellman(&public_key);

    // HKDF derivation
    let hkdf = Hkdf::<Sha256>::new(None, shared_secret.as_bytes());
    let mut final_key = [0u8; 32];
    hkdf.expand(b"ECDH_v1", &mut final_key)
        .map_err(|e| anyhow!("HKDF derivation failed: {}", e))?;

    Ok(final_key)
}

fn find_min_allowed_total_size(min_data_len: usize) -> Result<usize> {
    for &size in ALLOWED_TOTAL_SIZES.iter() {
        if size >= min_data_len {
            return Ok(size);
        }
    }
    Err(anyhow!("data too large: {} bytes exceeds max allowed {}", min_data_len, SIZE_MAX))
}

// Message Encryption
pub fn encrypt_message(plaintext: &[u8], shared_secret: &[u8; 32], session_id: u64) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
    let cipher = XChaCha20Poly1305::new(shared_secret.into());
    
    // Create base frame
    let mut base_frame = vec![0u8; FRAME_HEADER_SIZE + plaintext.len()];
    
    // Magic bytes
    base_frame[0..2].copy_from_slice(&MAGIC_BYTES_VALUE.to_le_bytes());
    
    // Session ID
    base_frame[2..10].copy_from_slice(&session_id.to_le_bytes());
    
    // Payload length
    if plaintext.len() > u32::MAX as usize {
        return Err(anyhow!("plaintext too large: {} bytes exceeds max uint32", plaintext.len()));
    }
    base_frame[10..14].copy_from_slice(&(plaintext.len() as u32).to_le_bytes());
    
    // Copy plaintext
    base_frame[14..].copy_from_slice(plaintext);
    
    // Calculate total size
    let min_total_size = NONCE_SIZE + base_frame.len() + TAG_SIZE;
    let total_size = find_min_allowed_total_size(min_total_size)?;
    let encrypted_block_size = total_size - NONCE_SIZE;
    let frame_with_padding_size = encrypted_block_size - TAG_SIZE;
    
    // Create frame with padding
    let mut frame_with_padding = vec![0u8; frame_with_padding_size];
    frame_with_padding[..base_frame.len()].copy_from_slice(&base_frame);
    
    // Fill random padding
    if base_frame.len() < frame_with_padding_size {
        OsRng.fill_bytes(&mut frame_with_padding[base_frame.len()..]);
    }
    
    // Generate nonce
    let mut nonce_bytes = [0u8; NONCE_SIZE];
    OsRng.fill_bytes(&mut nonce_bytes);
    let nonce = XNonce::from_slice(&nonce_bytes);
    
    // Encrypt
    let ciphertext = cipher
        .encrypt(nonce, frame_with_padding.as_slice())
        .map_err(|e| anyhow!("encryption failed: {}", e))?;
    
    // Combine packet
    let mut packet = Vec::with_capacity(total_size);
    packet.extend_from_slice(&nonce_bytes);
    packet.extend_from_slice(&ciphertext);
    
    Ok(packet)
}

// Message Decryption
pub fn decrypt_message(packet: &[u8], shared_secret: &[u8; 32]) -> Result<(Vec<u8>, u64)> {
    // Validate packet size
    if !ALLOWED_TOTAL_SIZES.contains(&packet.len()) {
        return Err(anyhow!(
            "invalid packet size {}: must be one of {:?}",
            packet.len(),
            ALLOWED_TOTAL_SIZES
        ));
    }
    
    let cipher = XChaCha20Poly1305::new(shared_secret.into());
    
    if packet.len() < NONCE_SIZE + TAG_SIZE {
        return Err(anyhow!(
            "packet too short: {} bytes, need at least {}",
            packet.len(),
            NONCE_SIZE + TAG_SIZE
        ));
    }
    
    let nonce = XNonce::from_slice(&packet[..NONCE_SIZE]);
    let encrypted_block = &packet[NONCE_SIZE..];
    
    let frame_with_padding = cipher
        .decrypt(nonce, encrypted_block)
        .map_err(|e| anyhow!("decryption failed (wrong key or corrupted data): {}", e))?;
    
    if frame_with_padding.len() < FRAME_HEADER_SIZE {
        return Err(anyhow!("decrypted frame too short: {} bytes", frame_with_padding.len()));
    }
    
    // Check magic bytes
    let magic = u16::from_le_bytes([frame_with_padding[0], frame_with_padding[1]]);
    if magic != MAGIC_BYTES_VALUE {
        return Err(anyhow!(
            "invalid magic bytes: expected 0x{:04X}, got 0x{:04X}",
            MAGIC_BYTES_VALUE,
            magic
        ));
    }
    
    // Extract session ID
    let session_id = u64::from_le_bytes(frame_with_padding[2..10].try_into()?);
    
    // Get data length
    let data_len = u32::from_le_bytes(frame_with_padding[10..14].try_into()?) as usize;
    
    if data_len > frame_with_padding.len() - FRAME_HEADER_SIZE {
        return Err(anyhow!(
            "declared data length {} exceeds frame bounds {}",
            data_len,
            frame_with_padding.len() - FRAME_HEADER_SIZE
        ));
    }
    
    let message = frame_with_padding[FRAME_HEADER_SIZE..FRAME_HEADER_SIZE + data_len].to_vec();
    
    Ok((message, session_id))
}

// File Encryption
pub fn encrypt_file(plaintext: &[u8], shared_secret: &[u8; 32]) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
    let cipher = XChaCha20Poly1305::new(shared_secret.into());
    
    let mut nonce_bytes = [0u8; NONCE_SIZE];
    OsRng.fill_bytes(&mut nonce_bytes);
    let nonce = XNonce::from_slice(&nonce_bytes);
    
    let mut ciphertext = nonce_bytes.to_vec();
    let encrypted = cipher
        .encrypt(nonce, plaintext)
        .map_err(|e| anyhow!("file encryption failed: {}", e))?;
    ciphertext.extend_from_slice(&encrypted);
    
    Ok(ciphertext)
}

// File Decryption
pub fn decrypt_file(ciphertext: &[u8], shared_secret: &[u8; 32]) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
    let cipher = XChaCha20Poly1305::new(shared_secret.into());
    
    if ciphertext.len() < NONCE_SIZE + TAG_SIZE {
        return Err(anyhow!("ciphertext too short: {} bytes", ciphertext.len()));
    }
    
    let nonce = XNonce::from_slice(&ciphertext[..NONCE_SIZE]);
    let encrypted_data = &ciphertext[NONCE_SIZE..];
    
    let plaintext = cipher
        .decrypt(nonce, encrypted_data)
        .map_err(|e| anyhow!("file decryption failed: {}", e))?;
    
    Ok(plaintext)
}

// Ed25519 functions
use ed25519_dalek::{Signer, SigningKey, Verifier, VerifyingKey, Signature};
use hex;

pub fn generate_ed25519_key_from_seed(seed: &[u8]) -> Result<SigningKey> {
    if seed.len() != 32 {
        return Err(anyhow!("seed must be exactly 32 bytes, got {}", seed.len()));
    }
    let mut seed_array = [0u8; 32];
    seed_array.copy_from_slice(seed);
    Ok(SigningKey::from_bytes(&seed_array))
}

pub fn sign_ed25519(private_key: &SigningKey, message: &[u8]) -> String {
    let signature = private_key.sign(message);
    hex::encode(signature.to_bytes())
}

pub fn verify_ed25519(
    public_key: &VerifyingKey,
    message: &[u8],
    signature_hex: &str,
) -> Result<bool> {
    let signature_bytes = hex::decode(signature_hex)
        .map_err(|e| anyhow!("invalid signature hex: {}", e))?;
    let signature = Signature::from_slice(&signature_bytes)
        .map_err(|e| anyhow!("invalid signature: {}", e))?;
    
    Ok(public_key.verify(message, &signature).is_ok())
}

pub fn compute_identity_hash(public_key: &VerifyingKey) -> String {
    use sha2::Digest;
    let mut hasher = Sha256::new();
    hasher.update(public_key.as_bytes());
    let result = hasher.finalize();
    hex::encode(result)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use x25519_dalek::{PublicKey, StaticSecret};

    #[test]
    fn test_generate_shared_key() {
        let alice_secret = StaticSecret::random_from_rng(OsRng);
        let bob_secret = StaticSecret::random_from_rng(OsRng);
        
        let alice_public = PublicKey::from(&alice_secret);
        let bob_public = PublicKey::from(&bob_secret);
        
        let alice_shared = generate_shared_key(
            alice_secret.as_bytes(),
            bob_public.as_bytes(),
        ).unwrap();
        
        let bob_shared = generate_shared_key(
            bob_secret.as_bytes(),
            alice_public.as_bytes(),
        ).unwrap();
        
        assert_eq!(alice_shared, bob_shared);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_message_encryption_decryption() {
        let shared_secret = [42u8; 32];
        let plaintext = b"Hello, World!";
        let session_id = 12345;
        
        let packet = encrypt_message(plaintext, &shared_secret, session_id).unwrap();
        let (decrypted, decrypted_session_id) = decrypt_message(&packet, &shared_secret).unwrap();
        
        assert_eq!(plaintext.to_vec(), decrypted);
        assert_eq!(session_id, decrypted_session_id);
    }
}

=== ./src/storage/paths.rs ===
// src/storage/paths.rs
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use std::fs;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

pub struct SafeFSOps {
    root: PathBuf,
}

impl SafeFSOps {
    pub fn new(root: &str) -> Result<Self> {
        let clean_root = PathBuf::from(root).canonicalize()
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("cannot access storage root: {}", e))?;
        
        if !clean_root.is_dir() {
            return Err(anyhow!("storage root is not a directory: {:?}", clean_root));
        }
        
        Ok(Self { root: clean_root })
    }
    
    pub fn resolve_path(&self, user_path: &str) -> Result<PathBuf> {
        let clean_path = Path::new(user_path);
        let abs_path = self.root.join(clean_path);
        
        // Canonicalize parent directory to check for path traversal
        let parent = abs_path.parent()
            .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("invalid path"))?;
        
        let canonical_parent = if parent.exists() {
            parent.canonicalize()
                .map_err(|e| anyhow!("path resolution failed: {}", e))?
        } else {
            // If parent doesn't exist, find the closest existing ancestor
            let mut current = parent;
            let mut components = Vec::new();
            while !current.exists() {
                components.push(current.file_name()
                    .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("invalid path component"))?
                    .to_string_lossy()
                    .to_string());
                current = current.parent()
                    .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("no valid parent"))?;
            }
            let canonical = current.canonicalize()
                .map_err(|e| anyhow!("path resolution failed: {}", e))?;
            
            let mut result = canonical;
            for component in components.iter().rev() {
                result = result.join(component);
            }
            result
        };
        
        // Check if the path stays within root
        if !canonical_parent.starts_with(&self.root) {
            return Err(anyhow!("path traversal detected: {}", user_path));
        }
        
        Ok(abs_path)
    }
    
    pub fn safe_open_file(&self, user_path: &str) -> Result<std::fs::File> {
        let safe_path = self.resolve_path(user_path)?;
        std::fs::File::open(&safe_path)
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("cannot open {:?}: {}", safe_path, e))
    }
    
    pub fn safe_write_file(&self, user_path: &str, data: &[u8], perm: u32) -> Result<()> {
        let safe_path = self.resolve_path(user_path)?;
        
        // Ensure parent directory exists
        if let Some(parent) = safe_path.parent() {
            fs::create_dir_all(parent)
                .map_err(|e| anyhow!("cannot create directory {:?}: {}", parent, e))?;
        }
        
        use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
        let permissions = fs::Permissions::from_mode(perm);
        
        fs::write(&safe_path, data)
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("cannot write file {:?}: {}", safe_path, e))?;
        
        fs::set_permissions(&safe_path, permissions)
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("cannot set permissions {:?}: {}", safe_path, e))?;
        
        Ok(())
    }
    
    pub fn safe_read_file(&self, user_path: &str) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
        let safe_path = self.resolve_path(user_path)?;
        fs::read(&safe_path)
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("cannot read file {:?}: {}", safe_path, e))
    }
    
    pub fn safe_stat(&self, user_path: &str) -> Result<fs::Metadata> {
        let safe_path = self.resolve_path(user_path)?;
        fs::metadata(&safe_path)
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("cannot stat file {:?}: {}", safe_path, e))
    }
    
    pub fn ensure_dir(&self, dir_path: &str) -> Result<()> {
        let safe_path = self.resolve_path(dir_path)?;
        fs::create_dir_all(&safe_path)
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("cannot create directory {:?}: {}", safe_path, e))
    }
}

pub fn get_base_dir() -> Result<String> {
    use std::env;
    
    let exec_path = env::current_exe()
        .map_err(|e| anyhow!("unable to get executable path: {}", e))?;
    let exec_dir = exec_path.parent()
        .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("no parent directory"))?;
    
    let portable_flag = exec_dir.join(".portable");
    if portable_flag.exists() {
        // Portable mode
        let base_dir = exec_dir.join("data");
        fs::create_dir_all(&base_dir)
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("failed to create portable data directory: {}", e))?;
        return Ok(base_dir.to_string_lossy().to_string());
    }
    
    // Standard mode: XDG_DATA_HOME or ~/.local/share
    let data_home = if cfg!(target_os = "windows") {
        env::var("LOCALAPPDATA").unwrap_or_else(|_| {
            dirs::home_dir()
                .unwrap_or_default()
                .join("AppData")
                .join("Local")
                .to_string_lossy()
                .to_string()
        })
    } else {
        env::var("XDG_DATA_HOME").unwrap_or_else(|_| {
            dirs::home_dir()
                .unwrap_or_default()
                .join(".local")
                .join("share")
                .to_string_lossy()
                .to_string()
        })
    };
    
    let base_dir = PathBuf::from(data_home).join("lastchance");
    fs::create_dir_all(&base_dir)
        .map_err(|e| anyhow!("failed to create app data directory: {}", e))?;
    
    Ok(base_dir.to_string_lossy().to_string())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use std::fs;
    
    #[test]
    fn test_resolve_path() {
        let temp_dir = std::env::temp_dir().join("test_safe_fs");
        fs::create_dir_all(&temp_dir).unwrap();
        
        let safe_fs = SafeFSOps::new(temp_dir.to_str().unwrap()).unwrap();
        
        // Test normal path
        let path = safe_fs.resolve_path("test.txt").unwrap();
        assert!(path.starts_with(&temp_dir));
        
        // Test path traversal
        let result = safe_fs.resolve_path("../escape.txt");
        assert!(result.is_err());
        
        fs::remove_dir_all(&temp_dir).unwrap();
    }
}

=== ./src/storage/db.rs ===
// src/storage/db.rs
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};
use rusqlite::{params, Connection};
use std::path::Path;
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};

pub const DB_FILE_NAME: &str = "user.db";

// ============================================================
// DATA MODELS
// ============================================================

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Contact {
    pub hash: String,
    pub public_key: Vec<u8>,
    pub nickname: String,
    pub created_at: DateTime<Utc>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Message {
    pub id: i64,
    pub contact_hash: String,
    pub room_hash: String,
    pub direction: String, // "in" or "out"
    pub text: String,
    pub timestamp: DateTime<Utc>,
    pub status: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct FileRecord {
    pub file_hash: String,
    pub room_hash: String,
    pub file_name: String,
    pub file_size: i64,
    pub uploaded_at: DateTime<Utc>,
}

// ============================================================
// STORAGE
// ============================================================

pub struct Storage {
    db: Arc<Mutex<Connection>>,
}

impl Storage {
    pub fn new(storage_path: &str) -> Result<Self> {
        let db_path = Path::new(storage_path).join(DB_FILE_NAME);
        let conn =
            Connection::open(&db_path).map_err(|e| anyhow!("failed to open database: {}", e))?;

        // Enable WAL mode for concurrent access
        conn.execute_batch(
            "
            PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL;
            PRAGMA busy_timeout=5000;
            PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON;
        ",
        )
        .map_err(|e| anyhow!("failed to configure database: {}", e))?;

        let storage = Self {
            db: Arc::new(Mutex::new(conn)),
        };

        storage.init_tables()?;

        Ok(storage)
    }

    fn init_tables(&self) -> Result<()> {
        let conn = self.db.lock().unwrap();

        // Contacts table
        conn.execute_batch(
            "
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS contacts (
                hash TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                public_key BLOB NOT NULL,
                nickname TEXT NOT NULL,
                created_at DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
            );

            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS rooms (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                name TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
                contact_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
                created_at DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                FOREIGN KEY(contact_hash) REFERENCES contacts(hash)
            );

            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS messages (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                contact_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
                room_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
                direction TEXT NOT NULL,
                text TEXT NOT NULL,
                timestamp DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                status TEXT DEFAULT 'delivered',
                FOREIGN KEY(contact_hash) REFERENCES contacts(hash),
                FOREIGN KEY(room_hash) REFERENCES contacts(hash)
            );

            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS files (
                file_hash TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                room_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
                file_name TEXT NOT NULL,
                file_size INTEGER NOT NULL,
                uploaded_at DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                FOREIGN KEY(room_hash) REFERENCES contacts(hash)
            );

            CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_messages_contact ON messages(contact_hash);
            CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_messages_room ON messages(room_hash);
            CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_messages_timestamp ON messages(timestamp);
            CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_files_room ON files(room_hash);
            CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_files_uploaded ON files(uploaded_at);
        ",
        )
        .map_err(|e| anyhow!("failed to create tables: {}", e))?;

        Ok(())
    }

    // ============================================================
    // CONTACTS
    // ============================================================

    pub fn add_contact(&self, hash: &str, public_key: &[u8], nickname: &str) -> Result<()> {
        let conn = self.db.lock().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO contacts (hash, public_key, nickname) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3)",
            params![hash, public_key, nickname],
        )
        .map_err(|e| anyhow!("failed to add contact: {}", e))?;
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn get_contact(&self, hash: &str) -> Result<Contact> {
        let conn = self.db.lock().unwrap();
        let mut stmt = conn
            .prepare("SELECT hash, public_key, nickname, created_at FROM contacts WHERE hash = ?1")
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("failed to prepare query: {}", e))?;

        let contact = stmt
            .query_row(params![hash], |row| {
                let created_at: String = row.get(3)?;
                Ok(Contact {
                    hash: row.get(0)?,
                    public_key: row.get(1)?,
                    nickname: row.get(2)?,
                    created_at: DateTime::parse_from_rfc3339(&created_at)
                        .unwrap_or_else(|_| Utc::now().into())
                        .with_timezone(&Utc),
                })
            })
            .map_err(|_e| anyhow!("contact not found: {}", &hash[..hash.len().min(16)]))?;

        Ok(contact)
    }

    pub fn get_all_contacts(&self) -> Result<Vec<Contact>> {
        let conn = self.db.lock().unwrap();
        let mut stmt = conn.prepare(
            "SELECT hash, public_key, nickname, created_at FROM contacts ORDER BY created_at DESC"
        ).map_err(|e| anyhow!("failed to prepare query: {}", e))?;

        let contacts = stmt
            .query_map([], |row| {
                let created_at: String = row.get(3)?;
                Ok(Contact {
                    hash: row.get(0)?,
                    public_key: row.get(1)?,
                    nickname: row.get(2)?,
                    created_at: DateTime::parse_from_rfc3339(&created_at)
                        .unwrap_or_else(|_| Utc::now().into())
                        .with_timezone(&Utc),
                })
            })
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("failed to query contacts: {}", e))?
            .collect::<std::result::Result<Vec<_>, _>>()
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("failed to read contacts: {}", e))?;

        Ok(contacts)
    }

    pub fn get_contact_hash_by_nickname(&self, nickname: &str) -> Result<String> {
        let conn = self.db.lock().unwrap();

        // First try rooms table
        let mut stmt = conn
            .prepare("SELECT contact_hash FROM rooms WHERE name = ?1")
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("failed to prepare query: {}", e))?;

        if let Ok(hash) = stmt.query_row(params![nickname], |row| row.get::<_, String>(0)) {
            return Ok(hash);
        }

        // Fallback: search in contacts
        let mut stmt = conn
            .prepare("SELECT hash FROM contacts WHERE nickname = ?1")
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("failed to prepare query: {}", e))?;

        let hash = stmt
            .query_row(params![nickname], |row| row.get::<_, String>(0))
            .map_err(|_e| anyhow!("contact not found: {}", nickname))?;
        // Auto-create room entry
        conn.execute(
            "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO rooms (name, contact_hash) VALUES (?1, ?2)",
            params![nickname, hash],
        )
        .map_err(|e| anyhow!("failed to create room: {}", e))?;

        Ok(hash)
    }

    pub fn ensure_room(&self, nickname: &str, hash: &str) -> Result<()> {
        let conn = self.db.lock().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO rooms (name, contact_hash) VALUES (?1, ?2)",
            params![nickname, hash],
        )
        .map_err(|e| anyhow!("failed to ensure room: {}", e))?;
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn update_contact_nickname(&self, contact_hash: &str, new_nickname: &str) -> Result<()> {
        let conn = self.db.lock().unwrap();
        let affected = conn
            .execute(
                "UPDATE contacts SET nickname = ?1 WHERE hash = ?2",
                params![new_nickname, contact_hash],
            )
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("failed to update contact nickname: {}", e))?;

        if affected == 0 {
            return Err(anyhow!(
                "contact not found: {}",
                &contact_hash[..contact_hash.len().min(16)]
            ));
        }

        Ok(())
    }

    // ============================================================
    // MESSAGES
    // ============================================================

    pub fn save_message_with_room(
        &self,
        contact_hash: &str,
        direction: &str,
        room_hash: &str,
        text: &str,
    ) -> Result<()> {
        let conn = self.db.lock().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO messages (contact_hash, direction, room_hash, text, status) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4, 'delivered')",
            params![contact_hash, direction, room_hash, text],
        ).map_err(|e| anyhow!("failed to save message: {}", e))?;
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn save_message(&self, contact_hash: &str, direction: &str, text: &str) -> Result<()> {
        self.save_message_with_room(contact_hash, direction, contact_hash, text)
    }

    pub fn get_room_messages(&self, room_hash: &str, limit: i64) -> Result<Vec<Message>> {
        let conn = self.db.lock().unwrap();
        let mut stmt = conn
            .prepare(
                "SELECT id, contact_hash, direction, text, timestamp, status
             FROM messages
             WHERE room_hash = ?1
             ORDER BY timestamp DESC
             LIMIT ?2",
            )
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("failed to prepare query: {}", e))?;

        let messages = stmt
            .query_map(params![room_hash, limit], |row| {
                let timestamp: String = row.get(4)?;
                Ok(Message {
                    id: row.get(0)?,
                    contact_hash: row.get(1)?,
                    room_hash: room_hash.to_string(),
                    direction: row.get(2)?,
                    text: row.get(3)?,
                    timestamp: DateTime::parse_from_rfc3339(&timestamp)
                        .unwrap_or_else(|_| Utc::now().into())
                        .with_timezone(&Utc),
                    status: row.get(5)?,
                })
            })
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("failed to query messages: {}", e))?
            .collect::<std::result::Result<Vec<_>, _>>()
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("failed to read messages: {}", e))?;

        Ok(messages)
    }

    pub fn get_messages(&self, contact_hash: &str, limit: i64) -> Result<Vec<Message>> {
        self.get_room_messages(contact_hash, limit)
    }

    // ============================================================
    // FILES
    // ============================================================

    pub fn save_file_record(
        &self,
        file_hash: &str,
        room_hash: &str,
        file_name: &str,
        file_size: i64,
    ) -> Result<()> {
        let conn = self.db.lock().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO files (file_hash, room_hash, file_name, file_size, uploaded_at)
             VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)",
            params![file_hash, room_hash, file_name, file_size],
        )
        .map_err(|e| anyhow!("failed to save file record: {}", e))?;
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn get_file_record(&self, file_hash: &str) -> Result<FileRecord> {
        let conn = self.db.lock().unwrap();
        let mut stmt = conn
            .prepare(
                "SELECT file_hash, room_hash, file_name, file_size, uploaded_at
             FROM files WHERE file_hash = ?1",
            )
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("failed to prepare query: {}", e))?;

        let file = stmt
            .query_row(params![file_hash], |row| {
                let uploaded_at: String = row.get(4)?;
                Ok(FileRecord {
                    file_hash: row.get(0)?,
                    room_hash: row.get(1)?,
                    file_name: row.get(2)?,
                    file_size: row.get(3)?,
                    uploaded_at: DateTime::parse_from_rfc3339(&uploaded_at)
                        .unwrap_or_else(|_| Utc::now().into())
                        .with_timezone(&Utc),
                })
            })
            .map_err(|_e| {
                anyhow!(
                    "file record not found: {}",
                    &file_hash[..file_hash.len().min(16)]
                )
            })?;

        Ok(file)
    }

    pub fn get_room_files(&self, room_hash: &str) -> Result<Vec<FileRecord>> {
        let conn = self.db.lock().unwrap();
        let mut stmt = conn
            .prepare(
                "SELECT file_hash, room_hash, file_name, file_size, uploaded_at
             FROM files WHERE room_hash = ?1
             ORDER BY uploaded_at DESC",
            )
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("failed to prepare query: {}", e))?;

        let files = stmt
            .query_map(params![room_hash], |row| {
                let uploaded_at: String = row.get(4)?;
                Ok(FileRecord {
                    file_hash: row.get(0)?,
                    room_hash: row.get(1)?,
                    file_name: row.get(2)?,
                    file_size: row.get(3)?,
                    uploaded_at: DateTime::parse_from_rfc3339(&uploaded_at)
                        .unwrap_or_else(|_| Utc::now().into())
                        .with_timezone(&Utc),
                })
            })
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("failed to query room files: {}", e))?
            .collect::<std::result::Result<Vec<_>, _>>()
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("failed to read files: {}", e))?;

        Ok(files)
    }

    pub fn close(&self) -> Result<()> {
        // The connection will be closed when the Arc<Mutex<Connection>> is dropped
        Ok(())
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use std::fs;

    fn setup_test_db() -> (Storage, String) {
        let temp_dir =
            std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("test_db_{}", chrono::Utc::now().timestamp()));
        fs::create_dir_all(&temp_dir).unwrap();

        let storage = Storage::new(temp_dir.to_str().unwrap()).unwrap();
        (storage, temp_dir.to_string_lossy().to_string())
    }

    fn cleanup_test_db(path: &str) {
        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(path);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_add_and_get_contact() {
        let (storage, path) = setup_test_db();

        let hash = "a".repeat(64);
        let public_key = vec![1u8; 32];
        let nickname = "Test User";

        storage.add_contact(&hash, &public_key, nickname).unwrap();

        let contact = storage.get_contact(&hash).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(contact.hash, hash);
        assert_eq!(contact.public_key, public_key);
        assert_eq!(contact.nickname, nickname);

        cleanup_test_db(&path);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_save_and_get_messages() {
        let (storage, path) = setup_test_db();

        let hash = "b".repeat(64);
        storage.add_contact(&hash, &vec![2u8; 32], "Test").unwrap();

        storage.save_message(&hash, "in", "Hello").unwrap();
        storage.save_message(&hash, "out", "Hi!").unwrap();

        let messages = storage.get_room_messages(&hash, 10).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(messages.len(), 2);

        // Messages should be in descending order (newest first)
        assert_eq!(messages[0].text, "Hi!");
        assert_eq!(messages[1].text, "Hello");

        cleanup_test_db(&path);
    }
}

=== ./src/identity/mod.rs ===
// src/identity/mod.rs
use crate::storage::SafeFSOps;
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use bip39::Mnemonic;
use ed25519_dalek::SigningKey;
use hkdf::Hkdf;
use rand::{rngs::OsRng, RngCore};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
use x25519_dalek::{PublicKey, StaticSecret};

pub const IDENTITY_FILE_NAME: &str = "identity.json";

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Identity {
    pub nickname: String,
    pub seed_phrase: String,
    pub public_key: Vec<u8>,
    pub private_key: Vec<u8>,
    pub ed25519_public_key: Vec<u8>,
    pub ed25519_private_key: Vec<u8>,
    pub hash: String,
    pub created_at: i64,
    pub updated_at: i64,
}

pub struct IdentityManager {
    storage_path: String,
    safe_fs: Option<SafeFSOps>,
    cached_id: Option<Identity>,
}

impl IdentityManager {
    pub fn new(storage_path: String) -> Self {
        Self {
            storage_path,
            safe_fs: None,
            cached_id: None,
        }
    }

    fn init_safe_fs(&mut self) -> Result<()> {
        if self.safe_fs.is_none() {
            self.safe_fs = Some(SafeFSOps::new(&self.storage_path)?);
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn generate_new_identity(&mut self, nickname: &str) -> Result<Identity> {
        let mut entropy = [0u8; 32];
        OsRng.fill_bytes(&mut entropy);

        let mnemonic = Mnemonic::from_entropy(&entropy)
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("failed to generate mnemonic: {}", e))?;
        let seed_phrase = mnemonic.to_string();

        let seed = mnemonic.to_seed("");
        let (x_pub, x_priv, ed_pub, ed_priv, hash) = derive_keys_from_seed(&seed)?;

        let nickname = if nickname.is_empty() {
            format!("user_{}", &hash[..8])
        } else {
            nickname.to_string()
        };

        let now = chrono::Utc::now().timestamp();

        let identity = Identity {
            nickname,
            seed_phrase,
            public_key: x_pub,
            private_key: x_priv,
            ed25519_public_key: ed_pub,
            ed25519_private_key: ed_priv,
            hash,
            created_at: now,
            updated_at: now,
        };

        self.cached_id = Some(identity.clone());
        Ok(identity)
    }

    pub fn load_or_create(&mut self, nickname: &str) -> Result<Identity> {
        self.init_safe_fs()?;

        let safe_fs = self.safe_fs.as_ref().unwrap();
        match safe_fs.safe_stat(IDENTITY_FILE_NAME) {
            Ok(_) => self.load(),
            Err(_) => {
                let identity = self.generate_new_identity(nickname)?;
                self.save(&identity)?;
                Ok(identity)
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn save(&mut self, identity: &Identity) -> Result<()> {
        self.init_safe_fs()?;

        let data = serde_json::to_string_pretty(identity)
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("failed to marshal identity: {}", e))?;

        let safe_fs = self.safe_fs.as_ref().unwrap();
        safe_fs.safe_write_file(IDENTITY_FILE_NAME, data.as_bytes(), 0o600)?;

        self.cached_id = Some(identity.clone());
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn load(&mut self) -> Result<Identity> {
        self.init_safe_fs()?;

        let safe_fs = self.safe_fs.as_ref().unwrap();
        let data = safe_fs.safe_read_file(IDENTITY_FILE_NAME)?;

        let identity: Identity = serde_json::from_slice(&data)
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("failed to unmarshal identity: {}", e))?;

        self.cached_id = Some(identity.clone());
        Ok(identity)
    }

    pub fn update_nickname(&mut self, nickname: &str) -> Result<()> {
        if self.cached_id.is_none() {
            self.load_or_create(nickname)?;
        }

        if let Some(identity) = self.cached_id.as_mut() {
            identity.nickname = nickname.to_string();
            identity.updated_at = chrono::Utc::now().timestamp();
            let identity_clone = identity.clone();
            self.save(&identity_clone)?;
        }

        Ok(())
    }
}

fn derive_keys_from_seed(seed: &[u8]) -> Result<(Vec<u8>, Vec<u8>, Vec<u8>, Vec<u8>, String)> {
    // X25519 via HKDF
    let hkdf_x = Hkdf::<Sha256>::new(None, seed);
    let mut x_priv_raw = [0u8; 32];
    hkdf_x
        .expand(b"X25519_E2EE_Key_v1", &mut x_priv_raw)
        .map_err(|e| anyhow!("hkdf x25519: {}", e))?;

    let private_key = StaticSecret::from(x_priv_raw);
    let public_key = PublicKey::from(&private_key);

    let x25519_priv = private_key.to_bytes().to_vec();
    let x25519_pub = public_key.as_bytes().to_vec();

    // Ed25519 via HKDF
    let hkdf_e = Hkdf::<Sha256>::new(None, seed);
    let mut ed_seed = [0u8; 32];
    hkdf_e
        .expand(b"Ed25519_Auth_Key_v1", &mut ed_seed)
        .map_err(|e| anyhow!("hkdf ed25519: {}", e))?;

    let signing_key = SigningKey::from_bytes(&ed_seed);
    let verifying_key = signing_key.verifying_key();

    let ed25519_pub = verifying_key.as_bytes().to_vec();
    let ed25519_priv = signing_key.to_bytes().to_vec();

    // Hash = SHA256(Ed25519PublicKey)
    let mut hasher = Sha256::new();
    hasher.update(&ed25519_pub);
    let hash_result = hasher.finalize();
    let hash = hex::encode(hash_result);

    Ok((x25519_pub, x25519_priv, ed25519_pub, ed25519_priv, hash))
}

impl Identity {
    pub fn get_public_key_hex(&self) -> String {
        hex::encode(&self.public_key)
    }

    pub fn get_ed25519_public_key_hex(&self) -> String {
        hex::encode(&self.ed25519_public_key)
    }

    pub fn sign_auth(&self, timestamp: i64) -> Result<String> {
        if self.ed25519_private_key.len() != 32 {
            return Err(anyhow!(
                "ed25519 private key invalid size: {}",
                self.ed25519_private_key.len()
            ));
        }

        let mut key_bytes = [0u8; 32];
        key_bytes.copy_from_slice(&self.ed25519_private_key);
        let signing_key = SigningKey::from_bytes(&key_bytes);

        use ed25519_dalek::Signer;
        let message = format!("{}{}", timestamp, self.hash);
        let signature = signing_key.sign(message.as_bytes());

        Ok(hex::encode(signature.to_bytes()))
    }
}

=== ./src/network/config.rs ===
// src/network/config.rs
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use crate::storage::SafeFSOps;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Config {
    pub server_url: String,
    pub file_server_url: String,
    pub mask_header_name: String,
    pub mask_header_value: String,
}

impl Default for Config {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            server_url: "https://msg.example.com".to_string(),
            file_server_url: "https://files.example.com".to_string(),
            mask_header_name: "X-DNS-Connect".to_string(),
            mask_header_value: "We_Are_Not_Legion".to_string(),
        }
    }
}

impl Config {
    pub fn load(base_dir: &str) -> Result<Self> {
        let safe_fs = SafeFSOps::new(base_dir)?;
        let config_path = "global.conf";
        
        match safe_fs.safe_read_file(config_path) {
            Ok(data) => {
                let mut cfg: Config = serde_json::from_slice(&data)
                    .map_err(|e| anyhow!("invalid config file: {}", e))?;
                
                // Fill empty fields with defaults
                let defaults = Config::default();
                if cfg.server_url.is_empty() {
                    cfg.server_url = defaults.server_url;
                }
                if cfg.file_server_url.is_empty() {
                    cfg.file_server_url = defaults.file_server_url;
                }
                if cfg.mask_header_name.is_empty() {
                    cfg.mask_header_name = defaults.mask_header_name;
                }
                if cfg.mask_header_value.is_empty() {
                    cfg.mask_header_value = defaults.mask_header_value;
                }
                
                Ok(cfg)
            }
            Err(_) => {
                // File doesn't exist, create with defaults
                let default_cfg = Config::default();
                let json_data = serde_json::to_string_pretty(&default_cfg)
                    .map_err(|e| anyhow!("failed to marshal default config: {}", e))?;
                
                safe_fs.safe_write_file(config_path, json_data.as_bytes(), 0o600)?;
                
                Ok(default_cfg)
            }
        }
    }
}

=== ./src/network/sender.rs ===
// src/network/sender.rs
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use reqwest::Client;
use std::time::Duration;
use crate::network::Config;

pub struct Sender {
    client: Client,
    config: Config,
}

impl Sender {
    pub fn new(config: &Config) -> Self {
        let client = Client::builder()
            .timeout(Duration::from_secs(30))
            .pool_max_idle_per_host(10)
            .build()
            .unwrap_or_default();
        
        Self {
            client,
            config: config.clone(),
        }
    }
    
    pub async fn send_message(&self, target_hash: &str, packet: &[u8]) -> Result<()> {
        let url = format!("{}/push/{}", self.config.server_url, target_hash);
        
        let max_retries = 3;
        let base_delay = Duration::from_secs(1);
        
        for attempt in 0..=max_retries {
            let response = self.client
                .post(&url)
                .header("Content-Type", "application/octet-stream")
                .header(&self.config.mask_header_name, &self.config.mask_header_value)
                .header("User-Agent", "Mozilla/5.0 (X11; Ubuntu; Linux x86_64; rv:149.0) Gecko/20100101 Firefox/149.0")
                .body(packet.to_vec())
                .send()
                .await;
            
            match response {
                Ok(resp) => {
                    let status = resp.status();
                    
                    if status.is_success() {
                        return Ok(());
                    }
                    
                    if status.as_u16() == 429 {
                        // Rate limited
                        if attempt == max_retries {
                            let body = resp.text().await.unwrap_or_default();
                            return Err(anyhow!("rate limited after {} retries: {}", max_retries + 1, body));
                        }
                        
                        let retry_delay = base_delay * 2u32.pow(attempt as u32);
                        log::info!("[SENDER] Rate limited, retrying in {:?} (attempt {}/{})", 
                            retry_delay, attempt + 1, max_retries + 1);
                        
                        tokio::time::sleep(retry_delay).await;
                        continue;
                    }
                    
                    let body = resp.text().await.unwrap_or_default();
                    return Err(anyhow!("server returned {}: {}", status, body));
                }
                Err(e) => {
                    if attempt == max_retries {
                        return Err(anyhow!("request failed after {} retries: {}", max_retries + 1, e));
                    }
                    let delay = base_delay * 2u32.pow(attempt as u32);
                    tokio::time::sleep(delay).await;
                }
            }
        }
        
        Err(anyhow!("unexpected loop exit"))
    }
    
    pub async fn upload_file(&self, payload: &[u8], file_name: &str) -> Result<String> {
        if payload.len() > 100 * 1024 * 1024 {
            return Err(anyhow!("file too large: {} bytes (max 100MB)", payload.len()));
        }
        
        let url = format!("{}/upload", self.config.file_server_url);
        
        let upload_client = Client::builder()
            .timeout(Duration::from_secs(120))
            .build()
            .unwrap_or_default();
        
        let response = upload_client
            .post(&url)
            .header("Content-Type", "application/octet-stream")
            .header("X-File-Name", file_name)
            .header(&self.config.mask_header_name, &self.config.mask_header_value)
            .header("User-Agent", "Mozilla/5.0 (X11; Ubuntu; Linux x86_64; rv:149.0) Gecko/20100101 Firefox/149.0")
            .body(payload.to_vec())
            .send()
            .await
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("upload request failed: {}", e))?;
        
        if !response.status().is_success() {
            let status = response.status();
            let body = response.text().await.unwrap_or_default();
            return Err(anyhow!("upload failed: {} - {}", status, body));
        }
        
        let file_hash = response.text()
            .await
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("read response: {}", e))?;
        
        Ok(file_hash.trim().to_string())
    }
}

=== ./src/network/listener.rs ===
// src/network/listener.rs
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use ed25519_dalek::SigningKey;
use reqwest::Client;
use std::time::Duration;
use crate::network::Config;

pub struct Listener {
    client: Client,
    config: Config,
}

impl Listener {
    pub fn new(config: &Config) -> Self {
        let client = Client::builder()
            .timeout(Duration::from_secs(60))
            .build()
            .unwrap_or_default();
        
        Self {
            client,
            config: config.clone(),
        }
    }
    
    pub async fn pull_messages_with_auth(
        &self,
        identity_hash: &str,
        pub_key_hex: &str,
        signing_key: &SigningKey,
    ) -> Result<Vec<Vec<u8>>> {
        use ed25519_dalek::Signer;
        
        let now = chrono::Utc::now().timestamp();
        let message = format!("{}{}", now, identity_hash);
        let signature = signing_key.sign(message.as_bytes());
        let sig_hex = hex::encode(signature.to_bytes());
        
        let url = format!("{}/pull/{}", self.config.server_url, identity_hash);
        
        let response = self.client
            .get(&url)
            .header("X-Identity-Key", pub_key_hex)
            .header("X-Timestamp", now.to_string())
            .header("X-Signature", &sig_hex)
            .header(&self.config.mask_header_name, &self.config.mask_header_value)
            .header("Accept", "multipart/form-data")
            .header("User-Agent", "Mozilla/5.0 (X11; Arch Linux; rv:130.0) Gecko/20100101 Firefox/130.0")
            .send()
            .await
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("pull request failed: {}", e))?;
        
        let status = response.status();
        
        if status == reqwest::StatusCode::NO_CONTENT {
            return Ok(Vec::new());
        }
        
        if status == reqwest::StatusCode::TOO_MANY_REQUESTS {
            return Err(anyhow!("rate limited (429)"));
        }
        
        if status == reqwest::StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED {
            let body = response.text().await.unwrap_or_default();
            return Err(anyhow!("unauthorized (401): {}", body));
        }
        
        if !status.is_success() {
            let body = response.text().await.unwrap_or_default();
            return Err(anyhow!("server returned {}: {}", status, body));
        }
        
        // Parse multipart response
        let content_type = response.headers()
            .get("Content-Type")
            .and_then(|v| v.to_str().ok())
            .unwrap_or_default();
        
        if content_type.starts_with("multipart/") {
            // Parse multipart
            let bytes = response.bytes()
                .await
                .map_err(|e| anyhow!("read body: {}", e))?;
            
            // Simple multipart parsing (you might want to use a proper multipart crate)
            self.parse_multipart_response(&bytes)
        } else {
            // Fallback: read as plain binary
            let data = response.bytes()
                .await
                .map_err(|e| anyhow!("read body: {}", e))?;
            
            if data.is_empty() {
                Ok(Vec::new())
            } else {
                Ok(vec![data.to_vec()])
            }
        }
    }
    
    fn parse_multipart_response(&self, data: &[u8]) -> Result<Vec<Vec<u8>>> {
        // Extract boundary from content-type
        let data_str = String::from_utf8_lossy(&data[..data.len().min(1024)]);
        let boundary_marker = "boundary=";
        let boundary = data_str
            .find(boundary_marker)
            .map(|i| &data_str[i + boundary_marker.len()..])
            .and_then(|s| s.split(';').next())
            .and_then(|s| s.split('\r').next())
            .and_then(|s| s.split('\n').next())
            .unwrap_or("");
        
        if boundary.is_empty() {
            return Ok(vec![data.to_vec()]);
        }
        
        let boundary_bytes = format!("--{}", boundary).into_bytes();
        let mut messages = Vec::new();
        let mut pos = 0;
        
        while pos < data.len() {
            // Find boundary
            if let Some(idx) = find_subsequence(&data[pos..], &boundary_bytes) {
                let content_start = pos + idx + boundary_bytes.len();
                
                // Check if this is the end boundary
                if content_start + 2 <= data.len() && &data[content_start..content_start + 2] == b"--" {
                    break;
                }
                
                // Skip headers (until empty line)
                if let Some(header_end) = find_subsequence(&data[content_start..], b"\r\n\r\n") {
                    let content = content_start + header_end + 4;
                    
                    // Find next boundary
                    if let Some(next_boundary) = find_subsequence(&data[content..], &boundary_bytes) {
                        let message_end = content + next_boundary - 2; // -2 for \r\n before boundary
                        if message_end > content {
                            messages.push(data[content..message_end].to_vec());
                        }
                        pos = content + next_boundary;
                    } else {
                        break;
                    }
                } else {
                    break;
                }
            } else {
                break;
            }
        }
        
        Ok(messages)
    }
    
    pub async fn download_file(&self, file_hash: &str) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
        let url = format!("{}/download/{}", self.config.file_server_url, file_hash);
        
        let response = self.client
            .get(&url)
            .header(&self.config.mask_header_name, &self.config.mask_header_value)
            .header("User-Agent", "Mozilla/5.0 (X11; Arch Linux; rv:130.0) Gecko/20100101 Firefox/130.0")
            .send()
            .await
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("download request failed: {}", e))?;
        
        if !response.status().is_success() {
            let status = response.status();
            let body = response.text().await.unwrap_or_default();
            return Err(anyhow!("download failed: {} - {}", status, body));
        }
        
        let data = response.bytes()
            .await
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("read file data: {}", e))?;
        
        Ok(data.to_vec())
    }
}

fn find_subsequence(haystack: &[u8], needle: &[u8]) -> Option<usize> {
    if needle.is_empty() || haystack.len() < needle.len() {
        return None;
    }
    
    for i in 0..=haystack.len() - needle.len() {
        if &haystack[i..i + needle.len()] == needle {
            return Some(i);
        }
    }
    
    None
}

=== ./src/app.rs ===
// src/app.rs
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use ed25519_dalek::SigningKey;
use log::{info, warn};
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
use std::time::Duration;
use tokio::sync::mpsc;

use crate::crypto;
use crate::identity::{Identity, IdentityManager};
use crate::network::{Config, Listener, Sender};
use crate::storage::{Contact, Message, SafeFSOps, Storage};

// ============================================================
// EVENT SYSTEM
// ============================================================

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum AppEvent {
    NewMessage {
        room_hash: String,
        text: String,
        timestamp: i64,
        sender: String,
    },
    ContactImport {
        hash: String,
        x25519: String,
        ed25519: Option<String>,
        nickname: String,
    },
    ContactImportError {
        error: String,
    },
    UploadStarted {
        file_name: String,
    },
    UploadProgress {
        status: String,
        progress: i32,
    },
    UploadCompleted {
        file_name: String,
        file_hash: String,
        file_size: Option<i64>,
    },
    UploadError {
        error: String,
    },
    DownloadStarted,
    DownloadCompleted {
        file_path: String,
    },
    DownloadError {
        error: String,
    },
}

// ============================================================
// MAIN APP STRUCTURE
// ============================================================

pub struct App {
    config: Config,
    base_dir: String,

    storage: Arc<Storage>,
    identity_manager: Arc<Mutex<IdentityManager>>,
    pub identity: Identity,

    shared_secrets: Arc<Mutex<HashMap<String, [u8; 32]>>>,
    session_id: Arc<Mutex<u64>>,

    sender: Arc<Sender>,
    listener: Arc<Listener>,

    signing_key: SigningKey,
    ed25519_pub_key_hex: String,

    window_active: Arc<Mutex<bool>>,
    current_room_hash: Arc<Mutex<String>>,

    safe_fs: Arc<SafeFSOps>,

    event_sender: mpsc::UnboundedSender<AppEvent>,
}

impl App {
    pub fn new(
        config: Config,
        base_dir: String,
        event_sender: mpsc::UnboundedSender<AppEvent>,
    ) -> Result<Self> {
        let safe_fs = Arc::new(SafeFSOps::new(&base_dir)?);

        // Ensure base directory exists
        safe_fs.ensure_dir(".")?;

        // Initialize storage
        let storage = Arc::new(Storage::new(&base_dir)?);

        // Initialize identity
        let identity_manager = Arc::new(Mutex::new(IdentityManager::new(base_dir.clone())));
        let identity = {
            let mut manager = identity_manager.lock().unwrap();
            manager.load_or_create("")?
        };

        // Setup Ed25519 signing key
        if identity.ed25519_private_key.len() != 32 {
            return Err(anyhow!(
                "Invalid Ed25519 private key size — identity may be corrupted"
            ));
        }

        let mut signing_key_bytes = [0u8; 32];
        signing_key_bytes.copy_from_slice(&identity.ed25519_private_key);
        let signing_key = SigningKey::from_bytes(&signing_key_bytes);
        let ed25519_pub_key_hex = identity.get_ed25519_public_key_hex();

        // Initialize network
        let sender = Arc::new(Sender::new(&config));
        let listener = Arc::new(Listener::new(&config));

        // Load shared secrets
        let shared_secrets = Arc::new(Mutex::new(HashMap::new()));
        let current_room_hash = Arc::new(Mutex::new(String::new()));

        {
            let contacts = storage.get_all_contacts()?;
            let mut secrets = shared_secrets.lock().unwrap();

            for contact in &contacts {
                match crypto::generate_shared_key(&identity.private_key, &contact.public_key) {
                    Ok(secret) => {
                        secrets.insert(contact.hash.clone(), secret);
                    }
                    Err(e) => {
                        warn!(
                            "Failed to generate shared secret for {}: {}",
                            &contact.hash[..16.min(contact.hash.len())],
                            e
                        );
                    }
                }
            }

            // Set current room
            if !contacts.is_empty() {
                let mut current = current_room_hash.lock().unwrap();
                *current = contacts[0].hash.clone();
                drop(current);

                let _ = storage.ensure_room(&contacts[0].nickname, &contacts[0].hash);
            }
        }

        info!(
            "App initialized: nickname={}, hash={}...",
            identity.nickname,
            &identity.hash[..16.min(identity.hash.len())]
        );

        Ok(Self {
            config,
            base_dir,
            storage,
            identity_manager,
            identity,
            shared_secrets,
            session_id: Arc::new(Mutex::new(0)),
            sender,
            listener,
            signing_key,
            ed25519_pub_key_hex,
            window_active: Arc::new(Mutex::new(false)),
            current_room_hash,
            safe_fs,
            event_sender,
        })
    }

    pub fn emit_event(&self, event: AppEvent) {
        let _ = self.event_sender.send(event);
    }

    pub async fn start(&self) -> Result<()> {
        info!("App started");

        // Start message listener
        let listener = self.listener.clone();
        let identity_hash = self.identity.hash.clone();
        let identity_pub_key = self.identity.get_public_key_hex();
        let identity_priv_key = self.identity.private_key.clone();
        let signing_key = self.signing_key.clone();
        let shared_secrets = self.shared_secrets.clone();
        let storage = self.storage.clone();
        let window_active = self.window_active.clone();
        let current_room_hash = self.current_room_hash.clone();
        let _config = self.config.clone();
        let event_sender = self.event_sender.clone();

        tokio::spawn(async move {
            let mut interval = tokio::time::interval(Duration::from_secs(5));
            let base_interval = Duration::from_secs(5);
            let mut current_interval = base_interval;

            loop {
                interval.tick().await;

                match listener
                    .pull_messages_with_auth(
                        &identity_hash,
                        &identity_hash, // This should be ed25519 pub key
                        &signing_key,
                    )
                    .await
                {
                    Ok(messages) => {
                        if current_interval != base_interval {
                            current_interval = base_interval;
                            interval = tokio::time::interval(current_interval);
                        }

                        for packet in messages {
                            Self::handle_incoming_packet(
                                packet,
                                &shared_secrets,
                                &storage,
                                &window_active,
                                &current_room_hash,
                                &identity_hash,
                                &identity_pub_key,
                                &identity_priv_key,
                                &event_sender,
                            )
                            .await;
                        }
                    }
                    Err(e) => {
                        warn!("Pull error: {}", e);

                        // Implement backoff for rate limiting
                        if e.to_string().contains("rate limited") {
                            current_interval =
                                std::cmp::min(current_interval * 2, Duration::from_secs(60));
                            interval = tokio::time::interval(current_interval);
                            info!(
                                "Rate limited, increasing interval to {:?}",
                                current_interval
                            );
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        });

        Ok(())
    }

    async fn handle_incoming_packet(
        packet: Vec<u8>,
        shared_secrets: &Arc<Mutex<HashMap<String, [u8; 32]>>>,
        storage: &Arc<Storage>,
        window_active: &Arc<Mutex<bool>>,
        current_room_hash: &Arc<Mutex<String>>,
        identity_hash: &str,
        identity_pub_key: &str,
        identity_priv_key: &[u8],
        event_sender: &mpsc::UnboundedSender<AppEvent>,
    ) {
        let secrets = shared_secrets.lock().unwrap();

        let mut decrypted_message = None;
        let mut from_hash = String::new();

        // Try all known keys
        for (hash, secret) in secrets.iter() {
            if let Ok((decrypted, _)) = crypto::decrypt_message(&packet, secret) {
                decrypted_message = Some(decrypted);
                from_hash = hash.clone();
                break;
            }
        }

        // If not decrypted, try as self-message
        if decrypted_message.is_none() {
            drop(secrets);

            // Generate self shared key
            if let Ok(public_key) = hex::decode(identity_pub_key) {
                if let Ok(self_secret) = crypto::generate_shared_key(identity_priv_key, &public_key)
                {
                    if let Ok((decrypted, _)) = crypto::decrypt_message(&packet, &self_secret) {
                        decrypted_message = Some(decrypted);
                        from_hash = identity_hash.to_string();
                    }
                }
            }
        } else {
            drop(secrets);
        }

        let message = match decrypted_message {
            Some(m) => m,
            None => {
                warn!("Failed to decrypt incoming packet");
                return;
            }
        };

        // Get sender name
        let from_name = if from_hash == identity_hash {
            format!("{} (self)", &identity_hash[..8.min(identity_hash.len())])
        } else {
            match storage.get_contact(&from_hash) {
                Ok(contact) => contact.nickname,
                Err(_) => from_hash[..8.min(from_hash.len())].to_string(),
            }
        };

        // Save to DB
        let message_text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&message).to_string();
        let _ = storage.save_message_with_room(&from_hash, "in", &from_hash, &message_text);

        // Send event to UI
        let _ = event_sender.send(AppEvent::NewMessage {
            room_hash: from_hash.clone(),
            text: message_text.clone(),
            timestamp: chrono::Utc::now().timestamp(),
            sender: from_name.clone(),
        });

        // System notification
        if from_hash != identity_hash {
            let room_hash = current_room_hash.lock().unwrap().clone();
            let is_window_active = *window_active.lock().unwrap();

            if from_hash != room_hash || !is_window_active {
                let title = format!("📨 {}", from_name);
                let body: String = message_text.chars().take(100).collect();

                // Check if it's a file message
                let final_body = if body.starts_with("📎 Файл:") {
                    body.lines().next().unwrap_or(&body).to_string()
                } else {
                    body
                };

                Self::notify_user(&title, &final_body);
            }
        }
    }

    fn notify_user(title: &str, message: &str) {
        if let Err(e) = notify_rust::Notification::new()
            .summary(title)
            .body(message)
            .show()
        {
            warn!("Failed to show notification: {}", e);
        }
    }

    // ============================================================
    // API METHODS FOR UI
    // ============================================================

    pub fn get_profile(&self) -> serde_json::Value {
        serde_json::json!({
            "nickname": self.identity.nickname,
            "hash": self.identity.hash,
            "publicKey": self.identity.get_public_key_hex(),
            "ed25519PublicKey": self.ed25519_pub_key_hex,
            "seedPhrase": self.identity.seed_phrase,
        })
    }

    pub fn get_my_contact_link(&self) -> String {
        use url::form_urlencoded;

        let public_key_hex = self.identity.get_public_key_hex();
        let params = [
            ("hash", self.identity.hash.as_str()),
            ("x25519", public_key_hex.as_str()),
            ("ed25519", self.ed25519_pub_key_hex.as_str()),
            ("nickname", self.identity.nickname.as_str()),
        ];

        let encoded: String = form_urlencoded::Serializer::new(String::new())
            .extend_pairs(params.iter())
            .finish();

        format!("lastchance://contact?{}", encoded)
    }

    pub fn get_my_contact_json(&self) -> String {
        let data = serde_json::json!({
            "hash": self.identity.hash,
            "x25519": self.identity.get_public_key_hex(),
            "ed25519": self.ed25519_pub_key_hex,
            "nickname": self.identity.nickname,
        });

        serde_json::to_string_pretty(&data).unwrap_or_default()
    }

    pub fn get_contacts(&self) -> Result<Vec<Contact>> {
        self.storage.get_all_contacts()
    }

    pub fn add_contact(&self, hash: &str, pub_key_hex: &str, nickname: &str) -> Result<()> {
        let pub_key =
            hex::decode(pub_key_hex).map_err(|e| anyhow!("invalid public key hex: {}", e))?;

        if pub_key.len() != 32 {
            return Err(anyhow!(
                "public key must be 32 bytes, got {}",
                pub_key.len()
            ));
        }

        let shared_secret = crypto::generate_shared_key(&self.identity.private_key, &pub_key)?;

        self.storage.add_contact(hash, &pub_key, nickname)?;

        {
            let mut secrets = self.shared_secrets.lock().unwrap();
            secrets.insert(hash.to_string(), shared_secret);
        }

        let _ = self.storage.ensure_room(nickname, hash);

        let mut current = self.current_room_hash.lock().unwrap();
        if current.is_empty() {
            *current = hash.to_string();
        }

        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn update_nickname(&self, new_nickname: &str) -> Result<()> {
        let mut manager = self.identity_manager.lock().unwrap();
        manager.update_nickname(new_nickname)?;
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn update_contact_nickname(&self, contact_hash: &str, new_nickname: &str) -> Result<()> {
        if contact_hash == self.identity.hash {
            return Err(anyhow!(
                "cannot rename your own identity this way; use UpdateNickname()"
            ));
        }
        if new_nickname.is_empty() {
            return Err(anyhow!("nickname cannot be empty"));
        }
        self.storage
            .update_contact_nickname(contact_hash, new_nickname)
    }

    pub async fn send_message(&self, room_hash: &str, text: &str) -> Result<()> {
        let shared_secret = self.get_shared_secret(room_hash)?;

        let session_id = {
            let mut id = self.session_id.lock().unwrap();
            *id += 1;
            *id
        };

        let packet = crypto::encrypt_message(text.as_bytes(), &shared_secret, session_id)?;

        self.sender.send_message(room_hash, &packet).await?;

        self.storage
            .save_message_with_room(room_hash, "out", room_hash, text)?;

        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn send_file_native(&self, room_hash: &str, file_path: &str) -> Result<()> {
        use std::io::Read;

        let file_name = std::path::Path::new(file_path)
            .file_name()
            .and_then(|n| n.to_str())
            .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("invalid file path"))?;

        // Emit upload started event
        self.emit_event(AppEvent::UploadStarted {
            file_name: file_name.to_string(),
        });

        // Open file
        let mut file = self.safe_fs.safe_open_file(file_path)?;
        let mut file_data = Vec::new();
        file.read_to_end(&mut file_data)
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("failed to read file: {}", e))?;

        let file_size = file_data.len() as i64;

        // Emit progress event
        self.emit_event(AppEvent::UploadProgress {
            status: "Шифрование...".to_string(),
            progress: 30,
        });

        // Get shared secret
        let shared_secret = self.get_shared_secret(room_hash)?;

        // Encrypt file
        let encrypted_data = crypto::encrypt_file(&file_data, &shared_secret)?;

        // Emit progress event
        self.emit_event(AppEvent::UploadProgress {
            status: "Отправка на сервер...".to_string(),
            progress: 60,
        });

        // Upload file
        let file_hash = match self.sender.upload_file(&encrypted_data, file_name).await {
            Ok(hash) => hash,
            Err(e) => {
                self.emit_event(AppEvent::UploadError {
                    error: e.to_string(),
                });
                return Err(anyhow!("upload failed: {}", e));
            }
        };

        // Save file record
        self.storage
            .save_file_record(&file_hash, room_hash, file_name, file_size)?;

        // Emit progress event
        self.emit_event(AppEvent::UploadProgress {
            status: "Завершено".to_string(),
            progress: 100,
        });

        // Emit upload completed event
        self.emit_event(AppEvent::UploadCompleted {
            file_name: file_name.to_string(),
            file_hash: file_hash.clone(),
            file_size: Some(file_size),
        });

        // Send file notification message
        let message_text = format!(
            "📎 Файл: {}\nРазмер: {}\nХеш: {}",
            file_name,
            humanize_bytes(file_size as u64),
            file_hash
        );

        self.send_message(room_hash, &message_text).await?;

        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn download_and_save_file(
        &self,
        file_hash: &str,
        _suggested_file_name: &str,
        save_path: &str,
    ) -> Result<()> {
        // Emit download started event
        self.emit_event(AppEvent::DownloadStarted);

        // Download encrypted file
        let encrypted_data = match self.listener.download_file(file_hash).await {
            Ok(data) => data,
            Err(e) => {
                self.emit_event(AppEvent::DownloadError {
                    error: e.to_string(),
                });
                return Err(anyhow!("download failed: {}", e));
            }
        };

        // Get shared secret
        let room_hash = self.current_room_hash.lock().unwrap().clone();
        let shared_secret = self.get_shared_secret(&room_hash)?;

        // Decrypt file
        let file_data = match crypto::decrypt_file(&encrypted_data, &shared_secret) {
            Ok(data) => data,
            Err(e) => {
                self.emit_event(AppEvent::DownloadError {
                    error: e.to_string(),
                });
                return Err(anyhow!("decryption failed: {}", e));
            }
        };

        // Save file
        if let Err(e) = self.safe_fs.safe_write_file(save_path, &file_data, 0o600) {
            self.emit_event(AppEvent::DownloadError {
                error: e.to_string(),
            });
            return Err(anyhow!("save failed: {}", e));
        }

        // Emit download completed event
        self.emit_event(AppEvent::DownloadCompleted {
            file_path: save_path.to_string(),
        });

        Ok(())
    }

    fn get_shared_secret(&self, room_hash: &str) -> Result<[u8; 32]> {
        {
            let secrets = self.shared_secrets.lock().unwrap();
            if let Some(secret) = secrets.get(room_hash) {
                return Ok(*secret);
            }
        }

        // Check if self
        if room_hash == self.identity.hash {
            let secret =
                crypto::generate_shared_key(&self.identity.private_key, &self.identity.public_key)?;

            let mut secrets = self.shared_secrets.lock().unwrap();
            secrets.insert(room_hash.to_string(), secret);
            return Ok(secret);
        }

        // Load from storage
        let contact = self.storage.get_contact(room_hash)?;
        let secret = crypto::generate_shared_key(&self.identity.private_key, &contact.public_key)?;

        let mut secrets = self.shared_secrets.lock().unwrap();
        secrets.insert(room_hash.to_string(), secret);

        Ok(secret)
    }

    pub fn get_messages(&self, room_hash: &str, limit: i64) -> Result<Vec<Message>> {
        let mut messages = self.storage.get_room_messages(room_hash, limit)?;
        messages.reverse(); // Return in chronological order
        Ok(messages)
    }

    pub fn switch_room(&self, room_hash: &str) {
        let mut current = self.current_room_hash.lock().unwrap();
        *current = room_hash.to_string();
    }

    pub fn get_current_room(&self) -> String {
        self.current_room_hash.lock().unwrap().clone()
    }

    pub fn set_window_active(&self, active: bool) {
        let mut window_active = self.window_active.lock().unwrap();
        *window_active = active;
    }

    pub fn handle_scheme_url(&self, raw_url: &str) {
        info!("Scheme URL received: {}", raw_url);

        if !raw_url.starts_with("lastchance://contact?") {
            warn!("Unknown scheme URL");
            self.emit_event(AppEvent::ContactImportError {
                error: "Invalid contact link format".to_string(),
            });
            return;
        }

        let params_str = raw_url.trim_start_matches("lastchance://contact?");
        let params: HashMap<String, String> = url::form_urlencoded::parse(params_str.as_bytes())
            .into_owned()
            .collect();

        let hash = params.get("hash").map(|s| s.clone()).unwrap_or_default();
        let x25519 = params.get("x25519").map(|s| s.clone()).unwrap_or_default();
        let ed25519 = params.get("ed25519").map(|s| s.clone());
        let nickname = params
            .get("nickname")
            .map(|s| s.clone())
            .unwrap_or_default();

        if hash.is_empty() || x25519.is_empty() || nickname.is_empty() {
            self.emit_event(AppEvent::ContactImportError {
                error: "Missing required contact parameters".to_string(),
            });
            return;
        }

        self.emit_event(AppEvent::ContactImport {
            hash,
            x25519,
            ed25519,
            nickname,
        });
    }

    pub fn get_base_dir(&self) -> &str {
        &self.base_dir
    }

    pub fn get_config(&self) -> &Config {
        &self.config
    }

    pub fn get_storage(&self) -> &Arc<Storage> {
        &self.storage
    }

    pub fn get_safe_fs(&self) -> &Arc<SafeFSOps> {
        &self.safe_fs
    }
}

// ============================================================
// UTILITY FUNCTIONS
// ============================================================

fn humanize_bytes(bytes: u64) -> String {
    if bytes < 1024 {
        return format!("{} B", bytes);
    } else if bytes < 1024 * 1024 {
        return format!("{:.1} KB", bytes as f64 / 1024.0);
    } else if bytes < 1024 * 1024 * 1024 {
        return format!("{:.1} MB", bytes as f64 / (1024.0 * 1024.0));
    } else {
        return format!("{:.1} GB", bytes as f64 / (1024.0 * 1024.0 * 1024.0));
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use tempfile::tempdir;

    fn setup_test_app() -> (App, tempfile::TempDir, mpsc::UnboundedReceiver<AppEvent>) {
        let temp_dir = tempdir().unwrap();
        let base_dir = temp_dir.path().to_str().unwrap().to_string();
        let config = Config::default();
        let (event_sender, event_receiver) = mpsc::unbounded_channel();

        let app = App::new(config, base_dir, event_sender).unwrap();
        (app, temp_dir, event_receiver)
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_add_contact() {
        let (app, _temp_dir, _receiver) = setup_test_app();

        let hash = "a".repeat(64);
        let pub_key_hex = "b".repeat(64);
        let nickname = "Test User";

        app.add_contact(&hash, &pub_key_hex, nickname).unwrap();

        let contacts = app.get_contacts().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(contacts.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(contacts[0].hash, hash);
        assert_eq!(contacts[0].nickname, nickname);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_humanize_bytes() {
        assert_eq!(humanize_bytes(0), "0 B");
        assert_eq!(humanize_bytes(512), "512 B");
        assert_eq!(humanize_bytes(1024), "1.0 KB");
        assert_eq!(humanize_bytes(1024 * 1024), "1.0 MB");
        assert_eq!(humanize_bytes(1024 * 1024 * 1024), "1.0 GB");
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_get_profile() {
        let (app, _temp_dir, _receiver) = setup_test_app();

        let profile = app.get_profile();
        assert!(profile["nickname"].is_string());
        assert!(profile["hash"].is_string());
        assert!(profile["publicKey"].is_string());
        assert!(profile["ed25519PublicKey"].is_string());
        assert!(profile["seedPhrase"].is_string());
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_contact_link() {
        let (app, _temp_dir, _receiver) = setup_test_app();

        let link = app.get_my_contact_link();
        assert!(link.starts_with("lastchance://contact?"));
        assert!(link.contains("hash="));
        assert!(link.contains("x25519="));
        assert!(link.contains("ed25519="));
        assert!(link.contains("nickname="));
    }
}

    env_logger::init();
    
    iced::application(
        "Not LastChance",
        ui::app::LastChanceApp::update,
        ui::app::LastChanceApp::view,
